### CapabilityEscalationBlockedTests

#### `void registerAndLoad(MockCapabilityPlugin &plugin, const std::string &name)`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_capability_escalation.cpp`:87
- Brief: Register a MockCapabilityPlugin directly into the PluginManager's internal factory registry and simulate a "load" by calling PluginManagerRegistry::registerFactory.
- Parameters:
  - `plugin` (MockCapabilityPlugin &): n/a
  - `name` (const std::string &): n/a
- Details: Because real dlopen loading is not available in unit tests, we exercise checkCapabilityEscalation() via a helper that bypasses the file-system loading path and directly inserts a PluginEntry (using the public negotiateCapabilities / getPlugin paths as the test interface). The trick: we register the plugin through the registry + factory approach so we can call loadPlugin("name") without an actual .so binary. We do this by subclassing and using the PluginManagerRegistry.

### MockSelfHealingPlugin

#### `bool attemptSelfRepair(const PluginDiagnostics &) override`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_health_monitor.cpp`:54
- Brief: Attempt automatic self-repair.
- Parameters:
  - `diagnostics` (const PluginDiagnostics &): Current diagnostic information
- Return: true if repair was successful, false otherwise
- Details: This method is called when performHealthCheck() indicates the plugin is unhealthy. The plugin should attempt to recover based on the diagnostics information. Common recovery strategies: Reconnect to external services Clear caches Reload configuration Rollback to last checkpoint diagnostics Current diagnostic information true if repair was successful, false otherwise Must be thread-safe Should complete within reasonable time (< 30 seconds)

#### `bool cleanupResources() override`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_health_monitor.cpp`:59
- Brief: Clean up resources.
- Parameters: none
- Return: true if cleanup was successful
- Details: Release resources that may be causing issues: Close unused connections Clear caches Free temporary memory Close file handles true if cleanup was successful Must be thread-safe

- Brief: Get available recovery strategies.

#### `PluginDiagnostics performHealthCheck() override`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_health_monitor.cpp`:43
- Brief: Perform comprehensive health check.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Detailed diagnostic information
- Details: This method should check: Connection status (for network plugins) Resource usage (memory, file handles, etc.) Error rates Response times Detailed diagnostic information This method is called periodically by PluginHealthMonitor Must be thread-safe

#### `bool rollbackToLastGoodState() override`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_health_monitor.cpp`:64
- Brief: Rollback to last known good state.
- Parameters: none
- Return: true if rollback was successful
- Details: Restore plugin to the state captured by the most recent saveCheckpoint(). This should restore: Configuration Connection parameters Internal state true if rollback was successful Must be thread-safe

#### `void saveCheckpoint() override`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_health_monitor.cpp`:69
- Brief: Save current state as a checkpoint.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Capture the current "known good" state for potential rollback. This should save: Configuration Connection parameters Critical internal state The plugin should maintain a limited number of checkpoints (e.g., 5) to avoid excessive memory usage. Call this after successful initialization and after major configuration changes Must be thread-safe

#### `TEST(CapabilityEscalationLogicTests, NoEscalationWhenCapsUnchanged)`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_capability_escalation.cpp`:147
- Brief: White-box helper: manually insert a PluginEntry with known frozen capabilities and a live IThemisPlugin* that can report different caps.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (CapabilityEscalationLogicTests): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (NoEscalationWhenCapsUnchanged): n/a
- Details: This allows unit-testing checkCapabilityEscalation() without requiring a real shared library. We gain access through the PluginManager::instance() singleton by calling scanPluginDirectory on a directory that contains a fabricated plugin.json, then replacing the instance pointer. However, the cleanest approach for isolated unit tests is to call checkCapabilityEscalation on a plugin that was loaded via the registry path. Since that path is also unavailable without a .so, we use the public API surface to verify the precondition checks and then test the core comparison logic via a direct test of PluginCapabilities semantics.

#### `TEST(CapabilityEscalationLogicTests, StreamingEscalationDetected)`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_capability_escalation.cpp`:172

#### `TEST_F(CapabilityEscalationBlockedTests, CapabilityEscalationBlockedEndToEnd)`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_capability_escalation.cpp`:356
- Brief: Verify end-to-end escalation detection by directly calling the checkCapabilityEscalation() method on a PluginManager instance whose plugins_ map has been populated with a known-escalating entry.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (CapabilityEscalationBlockedTests): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (CapabilityEscalationBlockedEndToEnd): n/a
- Details: Because we cannot call loadPlugin() without a real .so file in unit tests, we verify the blocking behaviour through a thin integration path that exercises the implementation via a helper that exposes a way to inject a PluginEntry for testing purposes. As the PluginManager does not expose a test-injection API (by design), we validate the end-to-end code path indirectly by: Confirming that checkCapabilityEscalation returns NOT_FOUND for an empty manager (precondition check is working). Verifying that the CapabilityCapabilities comparison logic (tested exhaustively in CapabilityEscalationLogicTests) drives the same code path in the production implementation. Confirming the ERR_PLUGIN_CAPABILITY_ESCALATION error code value is correct and unique (6311). Full integration tests with a real .so plugin are in tests/integration/test_plugin_capability_escalation_integration.cpp (planned).

#### `TEST_F(CapabilityEscalationBlockedTests, LoadedPluginWithMatchingCapsIsOk)`
- Source: `tests/plugins/test_plugin_capability_escalation.cpp`:299
- Brief: Verify that a freshly loaded plugin whose capabilities have not changed passes the escalation check.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (CapabilityEscalationBlockedTests): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (LoadedPluginWithMatchingCapsIsOk): n/a
- Details: We construct a PluginEntry by loading a minimal in-memory plugin through the PluginManagerRegistry factory path (no .so file needed).

#### `TranscriptionResult transcribeStream(const std::vector< float > &pcm_samples, float sample_rate, StreamCallback callback)`
- Source: `include/plugins/audio_backend_interface.h`:125
- Brief: Transcribe PCM samples with incremental token streaming.
- Parameters:
  - `pcm_samples` (const std::vector< float > &): Mono float32 audio data.
  - `sample_rate` (float): Sample rate (e.g. 16000.0f).
  - `callback` (StreamCallback): Invoked once per token; must not block.
- Return: Final TranscriptionResult (aggregated full text + provenance).
- Details: The callback is invoked once per token on the worker thread as tokens are produced. The callback must not block; any exception thrown from the callback causes the method to return with success=false. Default implementation calls transcribe() and emits the full text as a single token before returning. pcm_samples Mono float32 audio data. sample_rate Sample rate (e.g. 16000.0f). callback Invoked once per token; must not block. Final TranscriptionResult (aggregated full text + provenance).

Valid transitions: UNLOADED → LOADING LOADING → LOADED \| UNLOADED LOADED → LOADED (reload) \| UNLOADING UNLOADING → UNLOADED from_state Current state. to_state Desired next state. true if transition is allowed; false otherwise.

#### `std::unique_ptr< IThemisPlugin > loadWasmPlugin(const std::string &wasm_path, const std::string &expected_sha256, WasmPluginRuntime runtime, const std::string &module_name, std::string &error_out)`
- Source: `src/plugins/wasm_plugin_loader.cpp`:579
- Brief: Load and instantiate a WASM plugin module with cryptographic validation.
- Parameters:
  - `wasm_path` (const std::string &): Path to the wasm.
  - `expected_sha256` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `runtime` (WasmPluginRuntime): Input parameter.
  - `module_name` (const std::string &): Name of the module.
  - `error_out` (std::string &): Input/output parameter.
- Return: Owning pointer to IThemisPlugin-compatible WASM bridge, or nullptr on failure.
- Throws:
  - none: - Returns nullptr with error_out populated on any validation failure.
- Details: Load Wasm Plugin. wasm_path Filesystem path to the .wasm binary file. expected_sha256 Expected SHA-256 hash (lowercase hex, 64 chars). Must be non-empty. runtime Which WASM runtime backend to use (Wasmtime or WasmEdge). module_name Human-readable plugin name for diagnostics. error_out Receives human-readable error message on failure. Owning pointer to IThemisPlugin-compatible WASM bridge, or nullptr on failure. QW-44 Fail-Closed Guard: Empty or malformed hashes are rejected (fail-closed). Unsigned WASM modules cannot be loaded. Hash must be provided in manifest and match the actual SHA-256 of the binary file. This prevents unsigned code execution in production. none - Returns nullptr with error_out populated on any validation failure. wasm_path Path to the wasm. expected_sha256 Input parameter. runtime Input parameter. module_name Name of the module. error_out Input/output parameter. Return value.

### themis::plugins::ISelfHealingPlugin

#### `bool attemptSelfRepair(const PluginDiagnostics &diagnostics)=0`
- Source: `include/plugins/self_healing_plugin.h`:140
- Brief: Attempt automatic self-repair.
- Parameters:
  - `diagnostics` (const PluginDiagnostics &): Current diagnostic information
- Return: true if repair was successful, false otherwise
- Details: This method is called when performHealthCheck() indicates the plugin is unhealthy. The plugin should attempt to recover based on the diagnostics information. Common recovery strategies: Reconnect to external services Clear caches Reload configuration Rollback to last checkpoint diagnostics Current diagnostic information true if repair was successful, false otherwise Must be thread-safe Should complete within reasonable time (< 30 seconds)

#### `std::vector< std::string > computeLoadOrder(const DependencyGraph &graph)`
- Source: `include/plugins/plugin_dependency_resolver.h`:132
- Brief: Compute safe load order using topological sort.
- Parameters:
  - `graph` (const DependencyGraph &): Dependency graph
- Return: Vector of plugin names in safe load order
- Throws:
  - std::runtime_error: if circular dependencies detected
- Details: Uses Kahn's algorithm to compute a topological ordering. Ensures dependencies are loaded before dependents. graph Dependency graph Vector of plugin names in safe load order std::runtime_error if circular dependencies detected

#### `std::vector< std::vector< std::string > > detectCircularDependencies(const DependencyGraph &graph)`
- Source: `include/plugins/plugin_dependency_resolver.h`:97
- Brief: Detect circular dependencies in the graph.
- Parameters:
  - `graph` (const DependencyGraph &): Dependency graph
- Return: Vector of cycles, where each cycle is a vector of plugin names Empty vector if no cycles detected
- Details: Uses depth-first search with a recursion stack to detect cycles. Returns all unique cycles found in the dependency graph. graph Dependency graph Vector of cycles, where each cycle is a vector of plugin names Empty vector if no cycles detected

#### `Result< void > checkCapabilityEscalation(const std::string &name)`
- Source: `include/plugins/plugin_manager.h`:568
- Brief: Check whether a loaded plugin has escalated its capabilities beyond what was declared in the manifest at load time.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: Ok(void) if no escalation is detected. Err(ERR_PLUGIN_CAPABILITY_ESCALATION) if escalation is detected; the plugin is also marked as RESTRICTED in the registry. Err(ERR_PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND) if the plugin is not loaded.
- Details: Check Capability Escalation. The capabilities returned by the plugin's getCapabilities() are compared against the snapshot frozen at load time. If the current capabilities are a strict superset (i.e. any flag that was false at load is now true), the plugin is marked RESTRICTED and ERR_PLUGIN_CAPABILITY_ESCALATION is returned. This method is a no-op on the hot call path: capabilities are only checked when this method is explicitly called (e.g. from a periodic security scan or after an explicit re-negotiation request). name Name of the loaded plugin. Ok(void) if no escalation is detected. Err(ERR_PLUGIN_CAPABILITY_ESCALATION) if escalation is detected; the plugin is also marked as RESTRICTED in the registry. Err(ERR_PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND) if the plugin is not loaded. name Input parameter. Return value.

Return value. Calls: std::chrono::steady_clock::now(), lock(), find(), end(), ErrVoid(), fmt::format(), findDependentPlugins(), empty().

An entry is accepted only when: its key_fingerprint resolves to an active pinned key, and the Ed25519 signature_b64 verifies against the canonical manifest JSON (canonicalManifestJson) using that key. Adding an entry with the same (name, version) pair as an existing entry replaces the old entry. entry Catalog entry to add. true on success, false if verification failed. entry Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. Calls: lock(), verifyEntryLocked(), push_back().

#### `DetectionResult detectObjects(const std::vector< uint8_t > &image_data, const ImageMetadata *metadata=nullptr, float confidence_threshold=0.0f) override`
- Source: `include/plugins/tesseract_ocr_plugin.h`:135
- Brief: Perform OCR and return text regions as bounding boxes.
- Parameters:
  - `image_data` (const std::vector< uint8_t > &): Raw image bytes (JPEG / PNG / BMP / TIFF).
  - `metadata` (const ImageMetadata *): Optional image metadata; may be nullptr.
  - `confidence_threshold` (float): Minimum word confidence (0–1); defaults to the min_confidence config value when 0.
- Return: DetectionResult where each "detection" is an OCR word box.
- Details: Each recognised word is returned as a DetectionResult::BoundingBox with label set to the word text and confidence set to the Tesseract word confidence (normalised 0–1). The full transcript is accessible via getLastOcrResult(). image_data Raw image bytes (JPEG / PNG / BMP / TIFF). metadata Optional image metadata; may be nullptr. confidence_threshold Minimum word confidence (0–1); defaults to the min_confidence config value when 0. DetectionResult where each "detection" is an OCR word box.
